缺氧诱导因子 1-α 抑制可减轻 H9N2 禽流感 病毒诱导的炎症反应

Hypoxia-inducible factor 1-alpha inhibition mitigates inflammatory responses induced by H9N2 avian influenza virus
作者： Suying Zhu, Beibei Li, Yaqi Wang, Yuhan Tian, Jiayue Zhang, Hongshan Wang
摘要中文翻译摘要 H9N2 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 对家禽和公共卫生构成重大威胁,并有可能导致毒性更强的流感病毒株的出现。缺氧诱导因子 1-α (HIF-1α) 在病毒 发病机制、免疫调节和炎症中发挥着至关重要的作用,使其成为治疗干预的有希望的靶点。在这里,我们探讨了 HIF-1α 在 H9N2 AIV 发病机制 中的参与以及相关的炎症反应。我们的数据显示,HIF-1α 通过调节线粒体健康度,加剧了 H9N2 AIV 感染引起的炎症。此外,我们还研究了 Sotetsuflavone (Seo)(一种天然衍生的黄酮类化合物),了解其调节 H9N2 感染后炎症反应的潜力。分子对接结合表面等离子共振测定表明 Seo 对 HIF-1α 表现出强大的结合亲和力,这意味着直接抑制机制。随后的体外研究表明,Seo 通过抑制感染 H9N2 AIV 的 HD11 细胞中的 HIF-1α 信号通路,大大减少了炎症细胞因子的产生,包括 IL-1β、IL-6 和 TNF-α。相应地,体内实验证实,Seo 给药显着改善 H9N2 感染小鼠的肺部炎症,同时还增强肺泡上皮修复,II 型肺泡上皮细胞标记物 Abca3 和 Sftpb 表达增加证明了这一点。总的来说,这些发现将 Seo 确定为 HIF-1α 的有效抑制剂,突显了其在减轻与流感病毒感染相关的炎症和肺损伤方面的治疗潜力。重要性 H9N2 禽流感 病毒通过缺氧诱导因子 1 α (HIF-1α) 介导的线粒体功能失调引发炎症性肺损伤。分子对接和表面等离子共振测定证实,天然类黄酮 Sotetsuflavone (Seo) 直接结合并抑制 HIF-1α。在 H9N2 感染的 HD11 细胞中,Seo 抑制 HIF-1α 信号传导,减少 IL-1β、IL-6 和 TNF-α 的产生。在体内,Seo 治疗 可减轻小鼠肺部炎症并增强肺泡上皮修复,上调 II 型肺细胞标记物(Abca3 和 Sftpb)。这些发现表明 Seo 是一种有前途的 HIF-1α 靶向疗法,用于治疗流感相关的免疫病理学。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在研究 HIF-1α 在 H9N2 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 发病机制 中的作用,并探索 Sotetsuflavone (Seo) 作为治疗剂的潜力。H9N2 AIV 对家禽和公共卫生构成重大威胁,并有可能导致毒性更强的流感病毒株的出现。
设计与对象
该研究探讨了 HIF-1α 在 H9N2 AIV 发病机制 中的参与以及体外和体内相关炎症反应。该研究是使用 HD11 细胞和 H9N2 感染的小鼠进行的。
核心方法
使用分子对接和表面等离子共振测定来确认 Seo 与 HIF-1α 的结合亲和力。使用感染H9N2 AIV的HD11细胞进行体外研究,并在感染H9N2的小鼠中进行体内实验。
主要结果
研究发现,Seo 通过抑制感染 H9N2 AIV 的 HD11 细胞中的 HIF-1α 信号通路,减少炎性细胞因子的产生,包括 IL-1β、IL-6 和 TNF-α。在体内,Seo 治疗 显着改善 H9N2 感染小鼠的肺部炎症并增强肺泡上皮修复。
解释与创新
研究结果表明 Seo 是 HIF-1α 的有效抑制剂,突显了其在减轻流感病毒感染相关炎症和肺损伤方面的治疗潜力。
科研与公卫意义
Seo 可能是一种很有前途的 HIF-1α 靶向疗法,用于治疗流感相关的免疫病理学,有可能降低肺损伤的严重程度并增强肺泡上皮修复。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到使用单一黄酮类化合物和单一 禽流感 病毒亚型的限制,这可能无法推广到其他亚型或物种。由于该研究的体外和体内实验性质,证据强度中等。

Hypoxia-inducible factor 1-alpha inhibition mitigates inflammatory responses induced by H9N2 avian influenza virus

Authors: Suying Zhu, Beibei Li, Yaqi Wang, Yuhan Tian, Jiayue Zhang, Hongshan Wang
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT H9N2 avian influenza virus (AIV) poses significant threats to poultry and public health, with the potential to contribute to the emergence of more virulent influenza strains. Hypoxia-inducible factor 1-alpha (HIF-1α) plays a crucial role in viral pathogenesis, immune regulation, and inflammation, making it a promising target for therapeutic intervention. Here, we explored the involvement of HIF-1α in H9N2 AIV pathogenesis and associated inflammatory responses. Our data revealed that HIF-1α exacerbates inflammation induced by H9N2 AIV infection through its regulation of mitochondrial fitness. Additionally, we investigated Sotetsuflavone (Seo), a naturally derived flavonoid, for its potential to modulate inflammatory responses following H9N2 infection. Molecular docking coupled with surface plasmon resonance assays indicated that Seo exhibits robust binding affinity to HIF-1α, implying a direct inhibitory mechanism. Subsequent in vitro studies demonstrated that Seo greatly reduced the production of inflammatory cytokines, including IL-1β, IL-6, and TNF-α, through suppressing the HIF-1α signaling pathway in HD11 cells infected with H9N2 AIV. Correspondingly, in vivo experiments established that Seo administration significantly ameliorated pulmonary inflammation in H9N2-infected mice, while also enhancing alveolar epithelial repair, as evidenced by increased expression of type II alveolar epithelial cell markers Abca3 and Sftpb. Collectively, these findings identify Seo as a potent inhibitor of HIF-1α, highlighting its therapeutic potential in mitigating inflammation and lung damage associated with influenza virus infection. IMPORTANCE H9N2 avian influenza virus triggers inflammatory lung injury via hypoxia-inducible factor-1 alpha (HIF-1α)-mediated dysregulation of mitochondrial function. The natural flavonoid Sotetsuflavone (Seo) directly binds and inhibits HIF-1α, as confirmed by molecular docking and surface plasmon resonance assays. In H9N2-infected HD11 cells, Seo suppresses HIF-1α signaling, reducing IL-1β, IL-6, and TNF-α production. In vivo , Seo treatment attenuates pulmonary inflammation and enhances alveolar epithelial repair in mice, upregulating type II pneumocyte markers (Abca3 and Sftpb). These findings identify Seo as a promising HIF-1α-targeted therapeutic for influenza-associated immunopathology.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the role of HIF-1α in H9N2 avian influenza virus (AIV) pathogenesis and to explore the potential of Sotetsuflavone (Seo) as a therapeutic agent. H9N2 AIV poses significant threats to poultry and public health, with the potential to contribute to the emergence of more virulent influenza strains.
Design and population
The study explored the involvement of HIF-1α in H9N2 AIV pathogenesis and associated inflammatory responses in vitro and in vivo. The research was conducted using HD11 cells and H9N2-infected mice.
Core methods
Molecular docking and surface plasmon resonance assays were used to confirm the binding affinity of Seo to HIF-1α. In vitro studies were conducted using HD11 cells infected with H9N2 AIV, and in vivo experiments were performed in H9N2-infected mice.
Main results
Seo was found to reduce the production of inflammatory cytokines, including IL-1β, IL-6, and TNF-α, through suppressing the HIF-1α signaling pathway in HD11 cells infected with H9N2 AIV. In vivo, Seo treatment significantly ameliorated pulmonary inflammation and enhanced alveolar epithelial repair in H9N2-infected mice.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings identify Seo as a potent inhibitor of HIF-1α, highlighting its therapeutic potential in mitigating inflammation and lung damage associated with influenza virus infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
Seo could be a promising HIF-1α-targeted therapeutic for influenza-associated immunopathology, potentially reducing the severity of lung damage and enhancing alveolar epithelial repair.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of a single flavonoid and a single avian influenza virus subtype, which may not generalize to other subtypes or species. The evidence strength is moderate due to the in vitro and in vivo experimental nature of the study.

牛中不同的流感界面:A(H5N1)病毒从野生鸟类到野生鸟类的反复跨物种溢出以及流感D病毒的流行性传播

Distinct influenza interfaces in cattle: repeated spillovers of highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) from wild birds and enzootic circulation of influenza D virus
作者： Wang S, Gao K, Li L, Chang Y, Mao L, Cheng S, Bai Y, Yang S, Li S, Guo Y
摘要中文翻译这篇迷你综述比较综合了 高致病性禽流感 A(H5N1) 和 D 型流感病毒 (IDV) 的流行病学、病理学、实验性 传播、基因组和 监测 证据,询问两种病毒的来源生态学、组织向性、脱落、传播、持久性和人畜共患证据有何不同。这些发现确定了暴露环境,但没有解决主要的自然牛到牛 传播 途径或病毒如何到达乳腺。摘要牛现在占据两个流行病学上不同的流感界面。基因不同的 H5N1 病毒已多次从 野生鸟类 传播到美国奶牛中。在泌乳奶牛中,感染主要发生在乳腺泡上皮,并在原料奶中产生高滴度的传染性病毒;牛群间的牛群流动通过乳制品生产网络传播了基因型 B3.13。在受影响的农场,牛奶和呼吸道样本中检测到了病毒 RNA;传染性病毒始终从原料奶和乳腺组织中回收,但仅从呼吸道或环境样本(例如挤奶厅空气和废水)中零星地回收。职业感染已经发生,但牛的持续适应或长期 储存宿主 状态尚未得到证实。相比之下,IDV 是一种适应牛的呼吸道病毒,通过直接接触和短程空气传播 传播 维持,IDV 基因组 片段之间存在谱系重配,并且经常与牛呼吸道疾病综合征 (BRDC) 相关,尽管 BRDC 中的独立因果作用尚未得到证实。人类血清反应性和人类气道模型中的有效复制表明暴露和生物相容性,但尚未证实有效的人类感染或...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解 高致病性禽流感 A(H5N1) 和 D 型流感病毒 (IDV) 的生态学、组织嗜性、脱落、传播、持久性和人畜共患潜力有何不同,重点是它们在牛体内的不同界面。
设计与对象
该研究涉及奶牛病例对照和 队列研究 研究,研究了 H5N1 和 IDV 与牛呼吸道疾病综合征 (BRDC) 和产奶量的流行病学和临床关联。它还包括跨不同地区和时间段的宏基因组分析和纵向 监测。
核心方法
该研究采用宏基因组测序、PCR、血清学 和实验性 传播 研究来研究犊牛的脱落、传播 和疾病背景。强调全 基因组 测序来追踪病毒运动和重配事件。
主要结果
在感染 H5N1 的奶牛的牛奶和呼吸道样本中检测到病毒 RNA,并且从原料奶和乳腺组织中始终回收到感染性病毒。对于 IDV,较高的病毒载量和合并感染与临床疾病相关,但在 BRDC 中没有独立的因果作用得到证实。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了 H5N1 和 IDV 的不同复制位点和 传播 动态,强调需要针对病毒的 传播 策略和风险框架。
科研与公卫意义
结果通过确定独特的暴露途径和 传播 途径,为 H5N1 和 IDV 的针对性 监测 和风险管理策略提供信息,特别是在乳制品生产系统和呼吸道疾病背景下。
局限与证据强度
由于 IDV 对横断面 PCR 和 血清学 的依赖,以及 H5N1 自然进入门户和 传播 途径尚未解决的不确定性,证据有限。信心是中等的。

Distinct influenza interfaces in cattle: repeated spillovers of highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) from wild birds and enzootic circulation of influenza D virus

Authors: Wang S, Gao K, Li L, Chang Y, Mao L, Cheng S, Bai Y, Yang S, Li S, Guo Y
Original abstract (English)
This Mini Review comparatively synthesizes epidemiological, pathological, experimental-transmission, genomic, and surveillance evidence for highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) and influenza D virus (IDV), asking how source ecology, tissue tropism, shedding, transmission, persistence, and zoonotic evidence differ between the two viruses. These findings identify exposure settings but do not resolve the dominant natural cow-to-cow transmission route or how the virus reaches the mammary gland. AbstractCattle now occupy two epidemiologically distinct influenza interfaces. Genetically distinct H5N1 viruses have spilled over repeatedly from wild birds into United States dairy cattle. In lactating cows, infection is prominent in mammary alveolar epithelium and produces high titers of infectious virus in raw milk; inter-herd cattle movements have disseminated genotype B3.13 through dairy production networks. On affected farms, viral RNA has been detected in milk and respiratory specimens; infectious virus is recovered consistently from raw milk and mammary tissues but only sporadically from respiratory or environmental samples such as milking-parlour air and wastewater. Occupational infections have occurred, but sustained cattle adaptation or long-term reservoir status has not been demonstrated. In contrast, IDV is a cattle-adapted respiratory virus maintained through direct contact and short-range airborne transmission, with interlineage reassortment among IDV genome segments and frequent association with bovine respiratory disease complex (BRDC), although an independent causal role in BRDC remains unproven. Human seroreactivity and efficient replication in human airway models indicate exposure and biological compatibility, but not confirmed productive human infection or...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand how the ecology, tissue tropism, shedding, transmission, persistence, and zoonotic potential of highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) and influenza D virus (IDV) differ, with a focus on their distinct interfaces in cattle.
Design and population
The study involved case-control and cohort studies in dairy cattle, examining the epidemiological and clinical associations of H5N1 and IDV with bovine respiratory disease complex (BRDC) and milk production. It also included metagenomic analyses and longitudinal surveillance across different regions and time periods.
Core methods
The study employed metagenomic sequencing, PCR, serology, and experimental transmission studies in calves to investigate shedding, transmission, and disease context. Whole-genome sequencing was emphasized to trace viral movement and reassortment events.
Main results
Viral RNA was detected in milk and respiratory specimens of H5N1-infected cows, with infectious virus consistently recovered from raw milk and mammary tissues. For IDV, higher viral loads and coinfections were associated with clinical disease, but no independent causal role in BRDC was confirmed.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the distinct replication sites and transmission dynamics of H5N1 and IDV, emphasizing the need for virus-specific surveillance strategies and risk frameworks.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results inform targeted surveillance and risk management strategies for H5N1 and IDV, particularly in dairy production systems and respiratory disease contexts, by identifying unique exposure pathways and transmission routes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by the reliance on cross-sectional PCR and serology for IDV, and by unresolved uncertainties regarding the natural portal of entry and transmission routes for H5N1. The confidence is moderate.

通用流感 疫苗 平台的最新进展:为通用 H5N1 疫苗 和大流行准备铺平道路

Recent advances in universal influenza vaccine platforms: paving the way for universal H5N1 vaccines and pandemic readiness
作者： Safdar Ali, Humaira Bibi, Hikmat Ullah, Muhammad Shahid Mehmood, Mohammad Azhar Kamal, Mohammad Jalal Nazari
摘要中文翻译摘要 H5N1 流感威胁是对世界人口整体健康的重大问题,它需要 疫苗 的开发和实施采取新的方法。前进的关键道路是开发通用 疫苗 平台,该平台提供针对各种 H5N1 毒株的长期交叉保护。这种类型的 疫苗 基于病毒的保守区域,对于提供针对抗原变异的长期免疫力是必要的。疫苗的未来应该基于产生细胞和体液免疫的能力,并提供针对各种病毒变种的最大程度的保护。同样重要的是,建立敏捷的制造能力,可以迅速扩大疫苗的产量,以满足全球在流感暴发的情况下的需求。mRNA 疫苗、病毒媒介或微针贴片技术可以快速适应新菌株,并在需要快速有效反应时有效应对情况。此外,公平的全球分配政策对于使中低收入国家的人口能够获得 疫苗 至关重要。国际应对措施需要注重公平,从而最大限度地减少获得 疫苗 的不平等。这是将全球通用的疫苗、交叉保护、敏捷生产和公平相结合的最终解决方案,以减少H5N1大流行的威胁。通过制定这些措施,国际社会可以为即将到来的流感 暴发 做好更充分的准备,并且可以及时、广泛和高效地获得 疫苗。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了 H5N1 流感对全球健康造成的重大威胁,以及在 疫苗 开发和实施中采取新方法来应对这一问题的需要。
设计与对象
该研究概述了 疫苗 开发的关键路径,重点关注通用 疫苗 平台,该平台提供针对各种 H5N1 毒株的长期交叉保护。
核心方法
该研究讨论了基于病毒保守区域的疫苗的必要性,以确保针对抗原变异的长期免疫。
主要结果
该研究强调,疫苗 的未来应该基于产生细胞和体液免疫,以提供针对各种病毒变体的最大保护。
解释与创新
作者解释说,mRNA 疫苗、病毒 媒介 或微针贴片可以快速适应新毒株,从而在流感 暴发 期间做出有效反应。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,建立敏捷制造能力对于确保在全球流感 暴发 期间及时、广泛和高效地提供 疫苗 至关重要。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告具体限制或证据强度评估。

Recent advances in universal influenza vaccine platforms: paving the way for universal H5N1 vaccines and pandemic readiness

Authors: Safdar Ali, Humaira Bibi, Hikmat Ullah, Muhammad Shahid Mehmood, Mohammad Azhar Kamal, Mohammad Jalal Nazari
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The H5N1 influenza threat is a significant issue to the overall health of the population in the world, and it demands new approaches in vaccine development and implementation. The critical path forward is the development of universal vaccine platforms, which provide long-term cross-protection against various H5N1 strains. This type of vaccines, based on conserved regions of viruses, is necessary to provide long term immunity against antigenic variation. The future of vaccines should be based on the ability to generate both cellular and humoral immunity and provide the greatest level of protection against a variety of viral variants. It is also crucial that agile manufacturing capacities are established, which can swiftly expand the production of vaccines to satisfy the demand in the world in the case of an influenza outbreak. The technology of mRNA vaccines, viral vectors, or microneedle patches can be adapted to novel strains rapidly and responds effectively to the situation when rapid and effective responses are required. Also, fair global distribution policies can be crucial to make it possible to make vaccines available to the population of low- and middle-income countries. The international response needs to focus on equity, whereby inequality in access to vaccines is minimized. This has been the ultimate solution of combining universal vaccines, cross-protection, agile production, and equity over the world to reduce the threat of H5N1 pandemic. By developing these measures, the world community can be more ready to the upcoming outbreak of influenza, and access to a vaccine can be timely, extensive, and efficient.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the significant global health threat posed by H5N1 influenza and the need for new approaches in vaccine development and implementation to combat this issue.
Design and population
The study outlines a critical path forward for vaccine development, focusing on universal vaccine platforms that provide long-term cross-protection against various H5N1 strains.
Core methods
The study discusses the necessity of vaccines based on conserved regions of viruses to ensure long-term immunity against antigenic variation.
Main results
The study emphasizes that the future of vaccines should be based on generating both cellular and humoral immunity to offer the greatest protection against a variety of viral variants.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that mRNA vaccines, viral vectors, or microneedle patches can be rapidly adapted to new strains, enabling effective responses during influenza outbreaks.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that establishing agile manufacturing capacities is essential to ensure timely, extensive, and efficient vaccine availability during global influenza outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations or evidence strength assessments.

澳大利亚的 H5N1:心理学、企鹅和防备。

H5N1 in Australia: psychology, penguins, and preparedness.
作者： Georgina Kenyon
摘要中文翻译澳大利亚南极局 (AAD) 宣布将于 2026 年 10 月从南极洲偏远的麦格理岛撤回所有工作人员,理由是如果目睹 H5N1(分支 2.3.4.4b 谱系)的 暴发 即将发生的大规模动物死亡,其工作人员可能会面临心理社会压力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了即将到来的 H5N1 暴发 对偏远地区工作人员的潜在心理社会影响,特别是在动物死亡和需要做好准备的情况下。
设计与对象
这项研究基于澳大利亚南极分部的一项公告,该分部决定于 2026 年 10 月从麦格理岛撤出工作人员,原因是目睹与 H5N1 相关的大规模动物死亡会带来预期的心理压力。
核心方法
这些方法涉及澳大利亚南极部门的行政决定,以评估和减轻心理社会风险,没有详细的实验或观察程序。
主要结果
主要结果是决定于 2026 年 10 月从麦格理岛撤出工作人员,理由是目睹与 H5N1 暴发 相关的大规模动物死亡可能会造成心理社会压力。
解释与创新
作者将这一决定解释为保护员工心理健康的一项主动措施,强调了将 H5N1 暴发 情景与偏远南极行动中的心理社会准备联系起来的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,在针对人畜共患病 暴发 的公共卫生准备中考虑心理社会影响的重要性,特别是在员工福祉至关重要的偏远环境中。
局限与证据强度
该证据基于公告,缺乏详细的实证数据,由于缺乏定量或定性分析,导致信心评估适中。

H5N1 in Australia: psychology, penguins, and preparedness.

Authors: Georgina Kenyon
Original abstract (English)
The Australian Antarctic Division (AAD) has announced it will withdraw all its staff from the remote Macquarie Island in Antarctica in October, 2026, citing the likelihood of psychosocial stress on its staff in the event of witnessing mass animal deaths from an impending outbreak of H5N1 (clade 2.3.4.4b lineage).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the potential psychosocial impact of an impending H5N1 outbreak on staff working in remote locations, particularly in the context of animal deaths and the need for preparedness.
Design and population
The study is based on an announcement by the Australian Antarctic Division regarding its decision to withdraw staff from Macquarie Island in October 2026, due to the anticipated psychological stress from witnessing mass animal deaths linked to H5N1.
Core methods
The methods involve an administrative decision by the Australian Antarctic Division to assess and mitigate psychosocial risks, without detailed experimental or observational procedures.
Main results
The main result is the decision to withdraw staff from Macquarie Island in October 2026, citing the likelihood of psychosocial stress from witnessing mass animal deaths associated with an H5N1 outbreak.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret this decision as a proactive measure to protect staff mental health, highlighting the novelty of linking H5N1 outbreak scenarios to psychosocial preparedness in remote Antarctic operations.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the importance of considering psychosocial impacts in public health preparedness for zoonotic outbreaks, particularly in isolated environments where staff well-being is critical.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on an announcement and lacks detailed empirical data, resulting in a moderate confidence assessment due to the absence of quantitative or qualitative analysis.

Nowcasting官方报告了异构报告延迟下的高致病性禽流感 暴发计数

Nowcasting officially reported highly pathogenic avian influenza outbreak counts under heterogeneous reporting delays
作者： Kun Xiang, Xiangbo Zhu
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决由于报告延迟而导致官方动物疾病系列不完整的挑战,特别是 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI)。该研究试图评估增加模型复杂性是否可以改善 暴发 等效计数的概率即时预报。
设计与对象
使用世界动物卫生组织 (WOAH) 世界动物卫生信息系统 (WAHIS) 的数据进行了一项回顾性观察研究,其中包括 46,860 例 HPAI 和 72,690 例非洲猪瘟 (ASF) 暴发。主要 HPAI 分析涉及来自 115 个国家 宿主 单位的 35,880 个观测值。
核心方法
使用加权区间评分 (WIS)、校准诊断、块引导、模拟和单独冻结的 USDA APHIS 转移评估,对 2024-2025 年 78 个锁定截止点对 12 种预先指定的方法进行了评估。作为方法的一部分,还测试了梯度增强分位数回归和零时的显式质量。
主要结果
在一周结束时,只有 7.6% 的最终 HPAI 和 6.7% 的 ASF 暴发 等效项可见,其完整性因 宿主、地区、年份、季节和工作量而异。延迟感知时间网络的得分比分层贝叶斯比较器差 6.9%,而关系图仅改变 WIS 0.4%。
解释与创新
预先指定的主要假设不受支持,这表明模型复杂性的增加并没有显着增强 暴发 等效计数的临近预报。该研究通过评估报告延迟对模型性能的影响引入了一种新颖的方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为 HPAI 暴发 开发更准确的临近预报模型提供了信息,这可以改进 监测 和响应策略。他们还强调了公共卫生和兽医科学报告延迟的重要性。
局限与证据强度
对于被排除在培训之外的高容量国家和地区,绩效显着恶化,这表明普遍性受到限制。由于该研究是基于历史数据和回顾性分析,因此证据是中等的。

Nowcasting officially reported highly pathogenic avian influenza outbreak counts under heterogeneous reporting delays

Authors: Kun Xiang, Xiangbo Zhu
Original abstract (English)
Official animal disease series are often incomplete due to delays in reporting. This retrospective observational study evaluated whether increased model complexity enhances probabilistic nowcasts of eventual officially reported outbreak-equivalent counts. We reconstructed historical information states from a fixed extraction from the World Organisation for Animal Health (WOAH) World Animal Health Information System (WAHIS), which included 46,860 highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) and 72,690 African swine fever (ASF) outbreaks. Twelve prespecified methods were assessed over 78 locked cutoffs for 2024–2025 using the Weighted Interval Score (WIS), calibration diagnostics, block bootstrap, simulation, and a separately frozen USDA APHIS transfer evaluation. The primary HPAI analysis included 35,880 observations from 115 country–host units. At week closure, only 7.6% of eventual HPAI and 6.7% of ASF outbreak equivalents were visible, with completeness varying by host, region, year, season, and workload. The prespecified primary hypothesis was not supported: the delay-aware temporal network scored 6.9% worse than the hierarchical Bayesian comparator, and the relational graph changed WIS by only 0.4%. Gradient-boosted quantile regression improved WIS by 18.5% (absolute difference −0.0397, 95% interval −0.0553 to −0.0255), but the improvement reached 26.0% among high-volume reporters and reversed to a 6.3% deficit among units with minimal reporting history. Conditioning on the partial count was 24.7% worse than a matched no-delay network at cohort age 0 and performed better thereafter. The slower-reporting ASF system reversed the pooled contrast, whereas the APHIS module improved WIS by 10.1% after system-specific retraining. Adding an explicit mass at zero enhanced the transparent comparator by 18.8% and resulted in no detectable difference from gradient boosting; the neural model remained 17.4% worse under the same recalibration. In exploratory analyses, gradient boosting cleared the prespecified 5% gate under all six cohort definitions and all three maturity horizons. Performance deteriorated markedly for high-volume countries and regions excluded from training. These estimates pertain to eventual officially reported outbreak equivalents under the surveillance conditions studied, not undetected infections.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to address the challenge of incomplete official animal disease series due to reporting delays, particularly for highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI). The research sought to evaluate whether increased model complexity could improve probabilistic nowcasts of outbreak-equivalent counts.
Design and population
A retrospective observational study was conducted using data from the World Organisation for Animal Health (WOAH) World Animal Health Information System (WAHIS), which included 46,860 HPAI and 72,690 African swine fever (ASF) outbreaks. The primary HPAI analysis involved 35,880 observations from 115 country–host units.
Core methods
Twelve prespecified methods were assessed over 78 locked cutoffs for 2024–2025 using the Weighted Interval Score (WIS), calibration diagnostics, block bootstrap, simulation, and a separately frozen USDA APHIS transfer evaluation. Gradient-boosted quantile regression and an explicit mass at zero were also tested as part of the methods.
Main results
At week closure, only 7.6% of eventual HPAI and 6.7% of ASF outbreak equivalents were visible, with completeness varying by host, region, year, season, and workload. The delay-aware temporal network scored 6.9% worse than the hierarchical Bayesian comparator, and the relational graph changed WIS by only 0.4%.
Interpretation and novelty
The prespecified primary hypothesis was not supported, indicating that increased model complexity did not significantly enhance the nowcasting of outbreak-equivalent counts. The study introduced a novel approach by evaluating the impact of reporting delays on model performance.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings inform the development of more accurate nowcasting models for HPAI outbreaks, which can improve surveillance and response strategies. They also highlight the importance of accounting for reporting delays in public health and veterinary science.
Limitations and evidence strength
Performance deteriorated markedly for high-volume countries and regions excluded from training, suggesting limitations in generalisability. The evidence is moderate, as the study is based on historical data and retrospective analysis.

特拉华州家禽生物安全 高致病性禽流感 存在的罕见事件模型 监测

Rare-event modelling of highly pathogenic avian influenza presence for poultry biosecurity surveillance in Delaware
作者： Chibuike Chiedozie Ibebuchi
摘要中文翻译高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 仍然是家禽生产和动物健康准备的主要威胁,但其稀有性使得空间一致的局部 监测 优先级变得困难。本研究开发了一个空间罕见事件建模框架,将稀疏 HPAI 检测转换为特拉华州 3 公里 监测 优先级分数,并评估了向马里兰州的邻近区域转移。使用禽舍基础设施、湿地和水文邻近性、农业用地覆盖、地形和野鸟发生预测因素对高致病性禽流感的存在进行建模。使用特拉华州的空间块验证来评估模型性能,并转移到单独的马里兰州网格。弹性网逻辑回归在接受者操作特征曲线 (AUC) 点估计下达到了最高的特拉华面积 0.935(95% 置信区间 [CI]:0.868–0.986),但区分度与岭逻辑回归在统计上没有区别。排名最高的 5%、10% 和 20% 特拉华细胞分别包含 11 个 HPAI 阳性细胞的 63.6%(95% CI:30.8-89.1%)、81.8%(48.2-97.7%)和 90.9%(58.7-99.8%)。在马里兰州,特拉华州训练的模型的 AUC 为 0.898 (95% CI: 0.719–0.984),并分别捕获了排名最高的 5% 和 10% 中 14 个 HPAI 阳性细胞的 64.3% (95% CI: 35.1–87.2%) 和 85.7% (57.2–98.2%)。家禽基础设施提供了大部分歧视(AUC = 0.930);添加环境预测因子将 AUC 增加到 0.935,这一差异在统计上与零无法区分。这些发现支持对家禽生物安全、外展和预防行动进行 监测 优先筛查,而不将疾病归因于各个农场。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 仍然是家禽生产和动物健康准备的主要威胁,但其稀有性使得空间一致的局部 监测 优先级变得困难。
设计与对象
本研究开发了一个空间罕见事件建模框架,将稀疏 HPAI 检测转换为特拉华州 3 公里 监测 优先级分数,并评估了向马里兰州的邻近区域转移。
核心方法
使用禽舍基础设施、湿地和水文邻近性、农业用地覆盖、地形和野鸟发生预测因素对高致病性禽流感的存在进行建模。
主要结果
排名最高的 5%、10% 和 20% 特拉华细胞分别包含 11 个 HPAI 阳性细胞的 63.6%(95% CI:30.8-89.1%)、81.8%(48.2-97.7%)和 90.9%(58.7-99.8%)。
解释与创新
这些发现支持对家禽生物安全、外展和预防行动进行 监测 优先筛查,而不将疾病归因于各个农场。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为针对家禽种群中高致病性禽流感的有针对性的生物安全措施和 监测 策略提供信息,从而增强准备和应对能力。
局限与证据强度
使用特拉华州的空间块验证评估模型性能,并转移到单独的马里兰州网格,但由于 HPAI 阳性细胞数量有限和潜在的区域变异性,证据强度中等。

Rare-event modelling of highly pathogenic avian influenza presence for poultry biosecurity surveillance in Delaware

Authors: Chibuike Chiedozie Ibebuchi
Original abstract (English)
Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) remains a major threat to poultry production and animal-health preparedness, but its rarity makes spatially consistent local surveillance prioritization difficult. This study developed a spatial rare-event modelling framework to transform sparse HPAI detections into 3-kilometre surveillance-priority scores in Delaware and evaluated adjacent-region transfer to Maryland. HPAI presence was modelled using poultry-house infrastructure, wetland and hydrographic proximity, agricultural land cover, topography, and wild-bird occurrence predictors. Model performance was assessed using spatial-block validation in Delaware and transfer to a separate Maryland grid. Elastic-net logistic regression achieved the highest Delaware area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) point estimate of 0.935 (95% confidence interval [CI]: 0.868–0.986), but discrimination was not statistically distinguishable from ridge logistic regression. The highest-ranked 5%, 10%, and 20% of Delaware cells contained 63.6% (95% CI: 30.8–89.1%), 81.8% (48.2–97.7%), and 90.9% (58.7–99.8%) of the 11 HPAI-positive cells, respectively. In Maryland, the Delaware-trained model achieved an AUC of 0.898 (95% CI: 0.719–0.984) and captured 64.3% (95% CI: 35.1–87.2%) and 85.7% (57.2–98.2%) of the 14 HPAI-positive cells in the highest-ranked 5% and 10%, respectively. Poultry infrastructure provided most of the discrimination (AUC = 0.930); adding environmental predictors increased AUC to 0.935, a difference not statistically distinguishable from zero. These findings support surveillance-priority screening for poultry biosecurity, outreach, and preventive action without attributing disease to individual farms.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) remains a major threat to poultry production and animal-health preparedness, but its rarity makes spatially consistent local surveillance prioritization difficult.
Design and population
This study developed a spatial rare-event modelling framework to transform sparse HPAI detections into 3-kilometre surveillance-priority scores in Delaware and evaluated adjacent-region transfer to Maryland.
Core methods
HPAI presence was modelled using poultry-house infrastructure, wetland and hydrographic proximity, agricultural land cover, topography, and wild-bird occurrence predictors.
Main results
The highest-ranked 5%, 10%, and 20% of Delaware cells contained 63.6% (95% CI: 30.8–89.1%), 81.8% (48.2–97.7%), and 90.9% (58.7–99.8%) of the 11 HPAI-positive cells, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings support surveillance-priority screening for poultry biosecurity, outreach, and preventive action without attributing disease to individual farms.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted biosecurity measures and surveillance strategies for HPAI in poultry populations, enhancing preparedness and response capabilities.
Limitations and evidence strength
Model performance was assessed using spatial-block validation in Delaware and transfer to a separate Maryland grid, but the evidence strength is moderate due to the limited number of HPAI-positive cells and potential regional variability.

非人灵长类动物检疫相关病毒病原体:病理学、诊断 监测、控制策略和一种健康影响

Quarantine-associated viral pathogens in non-human primates: pathobiology, diagnostic surveillance, control strategies, and one health implications
作者： Bae G, Jeon E, Mo J, Lee J, Hong J, Lee D, Koo B
摘要中文翻译本综述比较了 NHP 的国家检疫框架,检查了病原体的特定特征,并讨论了它们对检疫和 监测 的影响。NHP 研究的这些结果提供了证据,支持在 暴发 期间根据 WHO MEURI 指南等监管和伦理框架对这些干预措施进行临床转化和紧急使用 (8)。本综述概述了与 NHP 相关的国家检疫方案和主要病毒病原体。最近新出现的传染病的 暴发,以及神经学和其他生物医学研究的进展,增加了非人类灵长类动物 (NHP) 作为临床前模型的使用。它们与人类的系统发育和生理学相似性使它们对于转化研究很有价值,特别是在突发公共卫生事件期间。然而,NHP 可能含有多种病毒病原体,其中一些会带来人畜共患风险,并可能损害动物健康和实验重现性。因此,检疫程序、病原体 监测 和生物安全计划对于在研究中安全可靠地使用 NHP 至关重要。尽管有这种需要,将国家检疫政策与主要病毒病原体的生物学和流行病学特征及其与疾病 监测 和控制的相关性联系起来的全面审查仍然有限。我们强调需要采取一种基于风险的综合方法,将纵向微生物监测、多模式诊断评估、设施级响应程序、职业健康准备和国际统一的检疫标准结合起来。4 讨论 WHO 最近的病原体优先排序工作强调了家庭层面的准备和应对策略 (197)。这个概念是...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了对非人类灵长类动物 (NHP) 改进检疫和 监测 策略的需求,因为它们在临床前研究中的作用以及它们可能携带的病毒病原体带来的风险。
设计与对象
该研究是一项综述,比较了 NHP 的国家检疫框架,检查了病原体的特定特征,并讨论了它们对检疫和 监测 的影响。它重点关注韩国的政策和 NHP 中记录的病毒感染的全球分布。
核心方法
这些方法包括审查国家检疫政策、分析病原体具体特征以及检查不同国家的微生物 监测 标准。该研究还考虑了特定病毒病原体的生物学和流行病学特征。
主要结果
该审查强调了 NHP 中存在多种病毒病原体,包括狂犬病病毒、马尔堡病毒、埃博拉病毒、猴痘病毒和黄热病病毒。它还指出了这些感染的地理分布以及各国之间 监测 做法的差异。
解释与创新
作者解释说,监测 实践的异质性和高风险人畜共患病原体的存在需要采取更加综合、基于风险的检疫和 监测 方法。新颖之处在于强调统一的国际标准和协调的设施级响应。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为 NHP 制定更有效的检疫和 监测 策略提供了信息,这对于临床前研究至关重要。他们还强调了国际合作对于减轻人畜共患风险和确保研究结果可靠性的重要性。
局限与证据强度
由于物种覆盖范围不均匀、机构之间的异质性以及某些病毒病原体的数据不足,证据受到限制。这些局限性表明对得出的结论有一定的信心。

Quarantine-associated viral pathogens in non-human primates: pathobiology, diagnostic surveillance, control strategies, and one health implications

Authors: Bae G, Jeon E, Mo J, Lee J, Hong J, Lee D, Koo B
Original abstract (English)
This review compares national quarantine frameworks for NHPs, examines pathogen-specific features, and discusses their implications for quarantine and surveillance. These findings from NHP studies provided evidence supporting the clinical translation and emergency use of these interventions during outbreaks under regulatory and ethical frameworks such as the WHO MEURI guidelines (8). This review provides an overview of national quarantine protocols and major viral pathogens relevant to NHPs. Recent outbreaks of emerging infectious diseases, along with advances in neurological and other biomedical research, have increased the use of non-human primates (NHPs) as preclinical models. Their close phylogenetic and physiological similarities to humans make them valuable for translational research, especially during public health emergencies. However, NHPs can harbor a wide range of viral pathogens, some of which pose zoonotic risks and may compromise animal health and experimental reproducibility. Therefore, quarantine procedures, pathogen surveillance, and biosecurity programs are essential for the safe and reliable use of NHPs in research. Despite this need, comprehensive reviews linking national quarantine policies with the biological and epidemiological characteristics of major viral pathogens and their relevance to disease surveillance and control remain limited. We emphasize the need for an integrated, risk-based approach incorporating longitudinal microbiological monitoring, multimodal diagnostic assessment, facility-level response procedures, occupational health preparedness, and internationally harmonized quarantine standards. 4DiscussionRecent WHO pathogen prioritization efforts have emphasized family-level preparedness and response strategies (197). This concept is...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for improved quarantine and surveillance strategies for non-human primates (NHPs) due to their role in preclinical research and the risks posed by viral pathogens they may harbor.
Design and population
The study is a review comparing national quarantine frameworks for NHPs, examining pathogen-specific features, and discussing their implications for quarantine and surveillance. It focuses on the Republic of Korea's policies and the global distribution of documented viral infections in NHPs.
Core methods
The methods include a review of national quarantine policies, an analysis of pathogen-specific characteristics, and an examination of microbiological surveillance standards across different countries. The study also considers the biological and epidemiological features of specific viral pathogens.
Main results
The review highlights the presence of various viral pathogens in NHPs, including rabies virus, Marburg virus, Ebola virus, monkeypox virus, and yellow fever virus. It also notes the geographical distribution of these infections and the variability in surveillance practices among countries.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the heterogeneity in surveillance practices and the presence of high-risk zoonotic agents necessitate a more integrated, risk-based approach to quarantine and surveillance. The novelty lies in the emphasis on harmonized international standards and coordinated facility-level responses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the development of more effective quarantine and surveillance strategies for NHPs, which are critical for preclinical research. They also highlight the importance of international collaboration to mitigate zoonotic risks and ensure the reliability of research outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by uneven species coverage, heterogeneity among institutions, and insufficient data for certain viral pathogens. These limitations suggest a moderate confidence in the conclusions drawn.

从分子进化到学校卫生室:挪威社区为基础的公共卫生护士(helsesykepleiere)在跨物种流感传播的一体化健康沟通中的作用

From molecular evolution to the school health room: the role of community-based public health nurses (helsesykepleiere) in one health communication on cross-species influenza transmission in Norway
作者： Klein J
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨社区公共卫生护士在将跨物种流感 传播 的病毒学和流行病学证据转化为针对挪威儿童、青少年和家庭的可行公共卫生传播方面的作用。鉴于已记录的青少年“One Health”素养方面的差距以及改进风险沟通的需要,这一点至关重要。
设计与对象
该研究是一项小型综述,分析了跨物种流感 传播 的分子基础、挪威流行病学和 One Health 治理背景以及面向儿童和家庭的风险沟通框架。它重点关注挪威社区公共卫生护士 (helsesykepleiere) 的作用,他们通过 helsestasjon- 和 skolehelsetjeneste 为儿童和家庭提供近乎普遍的接触。
核心方法
这些方法包括综合有关 宿主 范围和传播性分子决定因素的现有文献,回顾挪威 One Health 治理背景,并检查已建立的儿童和家庭风险沟通框架。该研究还分析了 监测 数据和机构对人畜共患流感防范的承诺。
主要结果
该研究报告称,分支 2.3.4.4b 病毒已成为全球主导病毒,在挪威野鸟和哺乳动物 监测 项目中反复检测到,包括狐狸和海豹中的 跨物种溢出。它还强调了在美国奶牛和伴侣动物中检测到这些病毒,并记录了与动物接触有关的 HPAI A(H5) 感染人类病例。
解释与创新
作者解释说,helsesykepleiere 由于与儿童和家庭几乎普遍接触,因此具有独特的优势,可以将 One Health 监测 的研究结果转化为家庭层面的指导。这是利用社区卫生人员进行有效风险沟通的新颖视角。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,将社区公共卫生护士纳入 One Health 沟通框架可以改善家庭层面对人畜共患流感的了解和保护行为,特别是在 2.3.4.4b 病毒分支全球流行的背景下。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于对现有文献和 监测 数据的小型回顾,这可能会限制分析的深度。然而,证据基于记录的流行病学趋势和机构承诺,支持适度的信心评估。

From molecular evolution to the school health room: the role of community-based public health nurses (helsesykepleiere) in one health communication on cross-species influenza transmission in Norway

Authors: Klein J
Original abstract (English)
This mini review synthesizes current literature on the molecular basis of influenza cross-species transmission, the Norwegian epidemiological and One Health governance context, and established child- and family-oriented risk-communication frameworks, to argue that Norway’s community-based public health nurses (helsesykepleiere) — who deliver near-universal, statutorily mandated contact with children and families through the helsestasjon- and skolehelsetjeneste — occupy a distinctive but underused position as One Health communication nodes (Helsedirektoratet, 2021). This mini review synthesizes current literature on (i) the molecular mechanisms underlying influenza A virus cross-species transmission, (ii) the global and Norwegian epidemiological landscape, (iii) Norwegian One Health governance and pandemic-preparedness structures, and (iv) established risk-communication and health-literacy frameworks for children and families, in order to outline a practical framework through which public health nurses can function as One Health communication nodes.Evidence for this mini review was identified through structured searches of PubMed/MEDLINE, Web of Science, and Google Scholar for English-language literature published between January 2020 and August 2026, using combinations of the terms ‘H5N1’, ‘avian influenza’, ‘clade 2.3.4.4b’, ‘cross-species transmission’, ‘reassortment’, ‘One Health’, ‘risk communication’, ‘health literacy’, ‘helsesykepleier’, and ‘public health nursing’, supplemented by hand-searched reference lists and direct searches of institutional sources (FHI, Norwegian Veterinary Institute, Mattilsynet, WHO, EFSA, and CDC) for current risk assessments and guidance. AbstractHighly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b viruses have undergone an...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the role of community-based public health nurses in translating virological and epidemiological evidence on cross-species influenza transmission into actionable public health communication for children, adolescents, and families in Norway. This is critical given the documented gaps in adolescent One Health literacy and the need for improved risk communication.
Design and population
The study is a mini review analyzing the molecular basis of influenza cross-species transmission, the Norwegian epidemiological and One Health governance context, and child- and family-oriented risk-communication frameworks. It focuses on the role of community-based public health nurses (helsesykepleiere) in Norway, who provide near-universal contact with children and families through the helsestasjon- and skolehelsetjeneste.
Core methods
The methods involved synthesizing current literature on molecular determinants of host range and transmissibility, reviewing the Norwegian One Health governance context, and examining established risk-communication frameworks for children and families. The study also analyzed surveillance data and institutional commitments to zoonotic influenza preparedness.
Main results
The study reports that clade 2.3.4.4b viruses have become globally dominant, with recurrent detections in Norwegian wild-bird and mammalian surveillance programs, including spillover into foxes and seals. It also highlights the detection of these viruses in U.S. dairy cattle and companion animals, with documented human cases of HPAI A(H5) infection linked to animal exposure.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the helsesykepleiere, due to their near-universal contact with children and families, are uniquely positioned to translate One Health surveillance findings into household-level guidance. This is a novel perspective on leveraging community-based health personnel for effective risk communication.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that integrating community-based public health nurses into One Health communication frameworks could improve family-level understanding and protective behavior against zoonotic influenza, particularly in the context of the ongoing global panzootic of clade 2.3.4.4b viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a mini review of existing literature and surveillance data, which may limit the depth of analysis. However, the evidence is grounded in documented epidemiological trends and institutional commitments, supporting a moderate confidence assessment.

针对流感血凝素头部-茎部界面一个保守的隐匿表位的抗体通过不同的结合模式发挥作用

Antibodies targeting a conserved cryptic epitope at the influenza hemagglutinin head-stem interface via distinct binding modes
作者： Guthrie L. Stroh, Hui-Bin Lv, Chun-Ke Chen, Yuan-Xin Sun, T. Pholcharee, Catherine Zhu, Shu-Ting Teo, Douglas A. Mitchell, C. Mok, Nicholas C. Wu
摘要中文翻译甲型流感病毒继续构成大流行威胁,最近高致病性 H5N1 病毒株在全球的传播就证明了这一点。因此,针对人畜共患流感亚型的 疫苗 的开发和表征已成为该领域的主要焦点。在这项研究中,我们表征了之前从 H5N1 疫苗 中分离出的两种具有广泛反应性的抗体。尽管它们具有不同的免疫球蛋白种系基因使用和结合模式,但冷冻电镜分析表明,两种抗体在血凝素 (HA) 头和干结构域的界面上共享高度保守的隐藏表位。该表位的抗原性受到 HA 亚基 1 残基 293 处天然氨基酸变异的影响,酸诱导的 HA 构象变化促进了其进入。然而,这两种抗体都不是中和性的,并且对体内季节性病毒和 H5N1 病毒仅具有有限的保护功效,因此代表了次优的 疫苗 反应。总体而言,我们的研究结果扩大了交叉反应抗体与未充分表征的 HA 表位的相互作用,并为下一代流感 疫苗 设计提供了见解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在表征针对禽流感 A病毒的广谱反应性抗体,因为这些病毒由于其全球传播,特别是高致病性H5N1毒株,持续构成大流行威胁。
设计与对象
该研究涉及表征先前从 H5N1 疫苗 中分离出的两种具有广泛反应性的抗体,重点关注它们与 禽流感 A 病毒和相关人畜共患病亚型的相互作用。
核心方法
该研究利用冷冻电镜分析来检查抗体的结合模式及其与 禽流感 A 病毒的血凝素 (HA) 头和干结构域的相互作用。
主要结果
尽管两种抗体具有不同的免疫球蛋白种系基因使用和结合模式,但它们在 HA 头-茎界面上共享一个高度保守的隐藏表位。
解释与创新
这些发现揭示了一个先前未被充分表征的 HA 表位,该表位是交叉反应抗体的目标,为下一代流感 疫苗 的开发提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以为针对 禽流感 A 病毒(特别是 H5N1)设计更有效的 疫苗 提供信息,通过靶向可能增强交叉反应性和保护功效的保守表位。
局限与证据强度
该研究的结果基于体内保护功效评估,其范围有限,可能无法完全捕捉 免疫应答 在不同 宿主 背景下的复杂性。

Antibodies targeting a conserved cryptic epitope at the influenza hemagglutinin head-stem interface via distinct binding modes

Authors: Guthrie L. Stroh, Hui-Bin Lv, Chun-Ke Chen, Yuan-Xin Sun, T. Pholcharee, Catherine Zhu, Shu-Ting Teo, Douglas A. Mitchell, C. Mok, Nicholas C. Wu
Original abstract (English)
Influenza A virus continues to pose pandemic threats, as demonstrated by the recent global spread of highly pathogenic H5N1 strains. Consequently, the development and characterization of vaccines targeting zoonotic influenza subtypes have become a major focus of the field. In this study, we characterize two broadly reactive antibodies previously isolated from H5N1 vaccinees. Although they have distinct immunoglobulin germline gene usage and binding modes, cryo-EM analysis shows that both antibodies share a highly conserved cryptic epitope at the interface of the hemagglutinin (HA) head and stem domains. The antigenicity of this epitope is influenced by natural amino acid variation at residue 293 of HA subunit 1, and its access is facilitated by acid-induced conformational change of HA. Nevertheless, both antibodies are non-neutralizing and possess only marginal protective efficacy against seasonal and H5N1 viruses in vivo, thus representing suboptimal vaccine responses. Overall, our findings expand the interactions of cross-reactive antibodies with an under-characterized HA epitope and provide insights for next-generation influenza vaccine design.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to characterize broadly reactive antibodies against avian influenza A viruses, as these viruses continue to pose pandemic threats due to their global spread, particularly with highly pathogenic H5N1 strains.
Design and population
The study involved characterizing two broadly reactive antibodies previously isolated from H5N1 vaccinees, focusing on their interaction with avian influenza A viruses and related zoonotic subtypes.
Core methods
The study utilized cryo-EM analysis to examine the binding modes of the antibodies and their interaction with the hemagglutinin (HA) head and stem domains of avian influenza A viruses.
Main results
Both antibodies share a highly conserved cryptic epitope at the HA head-stem interface, despite having distinct immunoglobulin germline gene usage and binding modes.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings reveal a previously under-characterized HA epitope that is targeted by cross-reactive antibodies, offering new insights for the development of next-generation influenza vaccines.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results can inform the design of more effective vaccines against avian influenza A viruses, particularly H5N1, by targeting conserved epitopes that may enhance cross-reactivity and protective efficacy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's results are based on in vivo protective efficacy assessments, which are limited in scope and may not fully capture the complexities of immune responses in different host contexts.

社论:传染病的数学建模和数据分析

Editorial: Mathematical Modelling and data analysis in infectious diseases
作者： Omame A, Xiao Y, Han Q, Iyaniwura S
摘要中文翻译这些论文共同强化了一个统一的信息:没有一种方法足以解决所有问题,正确的分析方法必须与当前的决策、数据生成过程以及推理错误的实际后果相匹配。它的统一信息很简单:没有一种方法足以解决所有问题。相反,方法的选择应该根据手头的决策、生成数据的过程、实际可用的观察结果以及不正确的推理或预测的后果来确定。他们的工作强调了实用的可识别性和数值稳定性,是将机械模型转化为可用于公共卫生决策的工具的先决条件。补充预测流将经典时间序列方法与非线性学习相结合。研究表明,社区间流动和基于学校的接触可以增加 暴发 概率并降低干预措施的有效性,而暴露后预防和主动病例发现的结合使用可大大降低跨场景的 暴发 风险。摘要本研究主题汇集了 22 项贡献,展示了目前用于理解和应对传染病威胁的数学和计算工具的广度。该研究主题涵盖机械建模、统计预测、机器学习、随机优化、计算流体动力学和负担分析,涵盖从 COVID-19 和流感到麻疹、腮腺炎和登革热等疾病。贡献集中在三个中心主题:从嘈杂的实时 监测 数据中提取稳健的、与决策相关的预测;模拟干预措施、移动性和连通性如何影响...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了从实时传染病数据中提取稳定的、与决策相关的预测的挑战,这对于有效的公共卫生 监测 和应对至关重要。该研究主题强调了传染病数据日益复杂以及对先进分析方法的需求。
设计与对象
该研究主题包括来自公共卫生、应用数学和微生物科学等各个领域的 22 篇贡献,涵盖了 COVID-19、流感、麻疹、腮腺炎和登革热等疾病。这些研究涵盖不同地点和人群,包括医疗机构、城市和国家。
核心方法
该研究采用了一系列方法,包括机械建模、统计预测、机器学习、随机优化和计算流体动力学。它还结合了流行病学分析、预测技术和荟萃分析方法来评估疾病负担和 传播 动态。
主要结果
结果表明,铁路连通性与 COVID-19 的早期城际传播有关,而武汉的封锁扰乱了这一途径。此外,社区间流动和基于学校的接触可以增加 暴发 概率并降低干预效果,而暴露后预防和主动病例发现可显着降低 暴发 风险。计算流体动力学模拟发现,由于风向和交叉通风,垂直建筑堆叠内的风险较高。
解释与创新
作者解释说,干预措施必须针对特定的连通性结构进行定制,例如铁路网络、建筑物和社会联系,这些结构创造了独特的 传播 路径。新颖之处在于展示这些因素如何影响 暴发 动态以及对上下文敏感模型的需求。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为基于连通性模式制定有针对性的公共卫生干预措施提供了信息,并强调了将环境和社会经济因素纳入疾病 监测 和负担估计以实现公平决策的重要性。
局限与证据强度
由于对实时数据的依赖(可能有噪音或不完整)以及需要对混合模型进行前瞻性评估,证据受到限制。这些研究表明对其结论有一定的信心,强调了上下文敏感的解释和校准的不确定性测量的重要性。

Editorial: Mathematical Modelling and data analysis in infectious diseases

Authors: Omame A, Xiao Y, Han Q, Iyaniwura S
Original abstract (English)
Together, these papers reinforce a unifying message: no single method suffices for every question, and the right analytical approach must be matched to the decision at hand, the data-generating process, and the practical consequences of getting the inference wrong. Its unifying message is simple: no single method suffices for every question. Instead, the choice of method should follow from the decision at hand, the process generating the data, the observations actually available, and the consequences of incorrect inference or prediction. Their work underscores practical identifiability and numerical stability as prerequisites for translating mechanistic models into tools that can be used operationally in public health decision-making.A complementary forecasting stream combines classical time-series methods with nonlinear learning. showed that inter-community mobility and school-based contact can increase outbreak probability and diminish the effectiveness of interventions, while the combined use of post-exposure prophylaxis and active case finding substantially reduces outbreak risk across scenarios.At a finer spatial scale, Chong et al. SummaryThis Research Topic brings together 22 contributions that showcase the breadth of mathematical and computational tools now used to understand and respond to infectious disease threats. Spanning mechanistic modeling, statistical forecasting, machine learning, stochastic optimization, computational fluid dynamics, and burden analysis, the Research Topic addresses diseases ranging from COVID-19 and influenza to measles, mumps, and dengue. Contributions cluster around three central themes: extracting robust, decision-relevant forecasts from noisy real-time surveillance data; modeling how interventions, mobility, and connectivity sha...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the challenge of extracting stable, decision-relevant predictions from real-time infectious-disease data, which is essential for effective public health surveillance and response. The Research Topic highlights the growing complexity of infectious-disease data and the need for advanced analytical methods.
Design and population
The Research Topic includes 22 contributions from various fields such as public health, applied mathematics, and microbial sciences, covering diseases like COVID-19, influenza, measles, mumps, and dengue. These studies span different locations and populations, including healthcare facilities, cities, and countries.
Core methods
The study employs a range of methods including mechanistic modeling, statistical forecasting, machine learning, stochastic optimization, and computational fluid dynamics. It also incorporates epidemiological analyses, forecasting techniques, and meta-analytical approaches to evaluate disease burden and transmission dynamics.
Main results
The results show that rail connectivity is associated with early intercity dissemination of COVID-19, and that the Wuhan lockdown disrupted this pathway. Additionally, inter-community mobility and school-based contact can increase outbreak probability and reduce intervention effectiveness, while post-exposure prophylaxis and active case finding significantly lower outbreak risk. Computational fluid dynamics simulations identified elevated risk within vertical building stacks due to wind direction and cross-ventilation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that interventions must be tailored to the specific structure of connectivity, such as rail networks, buildings, and social contacts, which create distinct transmission pathways. The novelty lies in demonstrating how these factors influence outbreak dynamics and the need for context-sensitive models.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the development of targeted public health interventions based on connectivity patterns and highlight the importance of integrating environmental and socioeconomic factors into disease surveillance and burden estimation for equitable policy-making.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by the reliance on real-time data, which may be noisy or incomplete, and the need for prospective evaluation of hybrid models. The studies demonstrate moderate confidence in their conclusions, emphasizing the importance of context-sensitive interpretation and calibrated uncertainty measures.

多价 抗病毒药物 缀合物通过交联病毒增强抗流感活性

Multivalent antiviral conjugates enhance activity against Influenza via cross-linking viruses
作者： Xun Feng, Lun-Zhi Yuan, Lei Zhao, Lian-Qi Liu, Fei Xie, Ming Zhou, Gong Cheng, Yi-Lei Guan, Ning-Shao Xia, Tong Cheng
摘要中文翻译摘要:长效药物-Fc 结合物​​ (CD388) 已成为抗甲型和乙型流感病毒的新型 抗病毒药物,但 Fc 结合如何重塑小分子功能的机制仍不清楚。在这里,我们通过点击化学建立了一个可编程多价 抗病毒药物 缀合物(AVC)平台,该平台能够灵活组合化合价和缀合药物,从而允许系统地询问化合价依赖性 抗病毒药物 机制。以扎那米韦为模型,单价、二价和三价扎那米韦与 Fc 缀合,其中三价扎那米韦-Fc 缀合物 (AVC-Z3) 表现出比低价 AVC 更高的活性。AVC-Z3 诱导病毒颗粒间聚集,从而将病毒进入的阻断与病毒释放的限制结合起来。这种交联介导的双阶段抑制代表了从神经氨酸酶抑制到物理病毒固定的机制转变。此外,合成了三价帕拉米韦-Fc缀合物(AVC-P3),并在体外表现出比AVC-Z3更强的抗病毒药物活性,体内半衰期比AVC-Z3和YTE 突变的CD388长得多。在 H1N1、H3N2、H5N1 和乙型流感感染小鼠模型中,AVC-P3 的体内抗病毒功效超过其他 AVC、巴洛沙韦和 CD388。总之,我们的研究结果将配体价态定义为 抗病毒药物 机制的决定因素。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在研究 Fc 结合重塑小分子 抗病毒药物 功能的机制,特别是在甲型和乙型流感病毒的背景下。这一背景凸显了了解长效药物-Fc 缀合物(例如 CD388)如何发挥抗流感作用的必要性,尽管它们具有潜力。
设计与对象
该研究利用点击化学建立了多价 抗病毒药物 缀合物 (AVC) 平台,以实现化合价和缀合药物的灵活组合。它专注于 禽流感 A 病毒,并评估了三价扎那米韦-Fc 缀合物 (AVC-Z3) 和三价帕拉米韦-Fc 缀合物 (AVC-P3) 的体外和体内 抗病毒药物 活性。
核心方法
研究人员使用点击化学创建了多价 抗病毒药物 缀合物,包括与 Fc 缀合的扎那米韦和帕拉米韦的单价、二价和三价版本。他们通过体外和体内实验评估了 抗病毒药物 的活性,将这些缀合物与其他 抗病毒药物 的功效进行比较,并评估其体内半衰期。
主要结果
三价扎那米韦-Fc 缀合物 (AVC-Z3) 表现出比较低价 AVC 更高的 抗病毒药物 活性。三价帕拉米韦-Fc 缀合物 (AVC-P3) 在体外表现出比 AVC-Z3 更强的 抗病毒药物 活性,与 YTE 突变 的 AVC-Z3 和 CD388 相比,在体内具有更长的半衰期。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,配体价决定了 抗病毒药物 机制,从神经氨酸酶抑制转变为通过交联进行物理病毒固定。这代表了 抗病毒药物 治疗的一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为针对 禽流感 A 病毒开发更有效的 抗病毒药物 策略提供了见解,可能改善人畜共患流感感染的 监测、诊断 和 治疗 选择。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于 禽流感 模型的体外和体内实验,证据基于单一模型系统。这些发现得到了直接实验结果的支持,表明有一定的信心。

Multivalent antiviral conjugates enhance activity against Influenza via cross-linking viruses

Authors: Xun Feng, Lun-Zhi Yuan, Lei Zhao, Lian-Qi Liu, Fei Xie, Ming Zhou, Gong Cheng, Yi-Lei Guan, Ning-Shao Xia, Tong Cheng
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Long-acting drug–Fc conjugate (CD388) has emerged as a new class of antivirals against influenza A and B, yet the mechanisms governing how Fc conjugation reshapes small-molecule function remain undefined. Here we establish a programmable multivalent antiviral conjugates (AVCs) platform by click chemistry that enables flexible combination of valencies and conjugated drugs, allowing systematic interrogation of valency-dependent antiviral mechanisms. Using zanamivir as a model, mono-, di-, and trivalent zanamivir are conjugated with Fc, with trivalent zanamivir-Fc conjugates (AVC-Z3) exhibiting higher activity than lower-valent AVCs. AVC-Z3 induces inter-virion aggregation, thereby coupling blockade of viral entry with restriction of viral release. This cross-linking–mediated dual-stage inhibition represents a mechanistic shift from neuraminidase suppression to physical viral immobilization. Furthermore, trivalent peramivir-Fc conjugate (AVC-P3) is synthesised and exhibits stronger antiviral activity than AVC-Z3 in vitro, with much longer half-life in vivo than AVC-Z3, and CD388 with YTE mutation. The in vivo antivirus efficacy of AVC-P3 surpasses other AVCs, baloxavir, and CD388 in H1N1-, H3N2-, H5N1-, and influenza B-infection mice models. Together, our findings define ligand valency as a determinant of antiviral mechanism.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the mechanisms by which Fc conjugation reshapes small-molecule antiviral function, particularly in the context of influenza A and B viruses. The background highlights the need to understand how long-acting drug-Fc conjugates, such as CD388, function against influenza, despite their potential.
Design and population
The study established a multivalent antiviral conjugates (AVCs) platform using click chemistry to enable flexible combination of valencies and conjugated drugs. It focused on avian influenza A viruses and evaluated the antiviral activity of trivalent zanamivir-Fc conjugates (AVC-Z3) and trivalent peramivir-Fc conjugates (AVC-P3) in vitro and in vivo.
Core methods
The researchers used click chemistry to create multivalent antiviral conjugates, including mono-, di-, and trivalent versions of zanamivir and peramivir conjugated with Fc. They assessed antiviral activity through in vitro and in vivo experiments, comparing the efficacy of these conjugates against other antivirals and evaluating their half-life in vivo.
Main results
Trivalent zanamivir-Fc conjugates (AVC-Z3) showed higher antiviral activity than lower-valent AVCs. Trivalent peramivir-Fc conjugates (AVC-P3) exhibited stronger antiviral activity than AVC-Z3 in vitro and had a much longer half-life in vivo compared to AVC-Z3 and CD388 with YTE mutation.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that ligand valency determines the antiviral mechanism, shifting from neuraminidase suppression to physical viral immobilization through cross-linking. This represents a novel approach to antiviral therapy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the development of more effective antiviral strategies for avian influenza A viruses, potentially improving surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment options for zoonotic influenza infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro and in vivo experiments with avian influenza models, and the evidence is based on a single model system. The findings are supported by direct experimental results, indicating moderate confidence.

美洲猪瘟和非洲猪瘟分子和血清学 诊断 的多实验室能力评估

Multi-laboratory proficiency assessment for molecular and serological diagnosis of classical and African swine fever in the Americas
作者： Onyilagha C, Hochman O, Brawerman G, Goonewardene K, Ambagala A
摘要中文翻译鉴于实验室之间的诊断分析性能可能存在差异,能力验证 (PT) 练习在确保实验室结果的质量和可靠性以及帮助实验室识别和解决程序问题方面发挥着关键作用。结果解读:X%≥50%,阳性;X% X < 40%,阴性;50% > x% ≥ 40%,可疑(ASF ELISA 试剂盒#1)。粗体,ASF 阳性;斜体,CSF 阳性;DPI,接种后天数;DPC,挑战后天数;阴性,阴性;Pos,阳性。ASFV 和 CSFV 的 PrimeStoreTM MTM 评估 基因组 稳定性与冻干血清不同,冻干血清通常在室温下短期稳定,通常持续几天到几周,因此适合在室温下运输,病毒核酸,尤其是病毒 RNA,在室温下超过 48 小时 (21) 就不稳定,如果在室温下运输,在参与测试时可能会导致假阴性结果实验室。参与、小组分布和结果报告已向美洲的区域和国家参考实验室以及当地兽医诊断实验室发出参加 2024 年 NCFAD 非洲猪瘟和猪瘟 PT 演习的邀请。非洲猪瘟 (ASF) 和古典猪瘟 (CSF) 是具有经济意义的猪跨境病毒性疾病,由于其对生产和国际贸易的严重影响,需要向世界动物卫生组织 (WOAH) 报告。两种疾病都具有相似的临床症状;因此,如果没有实验室诊断,就无法可靠地区分它们。作为 ASF 和 CSF 的 WOAH 参考实验室之一,国家外来动物疾病中心 (NCFAD) 组织了第一次 PT 演习...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估美洲猪瘟 (CSF) 和非洲猪瘟 (ASF) 的分子和血清学方法的诊断准确性,强调需要标准化测试以确保跨实验室获得可靠的结果。
设计与对象
进行了多实验室能力验证测试,涉及 15 个分子样本(6 个 ASF 和 7 个 CSF 病毒,加上 2 个阴性对照)和 20 个血清学样本(10 个 ASF 和 5 个 CSF 抗体)。该研究于 2024 年至 2026 年间在美洲进行,区域和国家参考实验室以及当地兽医诊断实验室参与其中。
核心方法
分子组是使用分别在猪原代白细胞和 PK-15 细胞系中繁殖的灭活 ASFV 和 CSFV 制备的。血清学组包括感染 ASFV、CSFV、BDV 或 BVDV 多种毒株的猪的血清。实时 PCR 和免疫过氧化物酶染色用于分子检测,而血清学检测则涉及 ELISA 试剂盒和验证性 IPT 测定。使用 MTM 在 治疗 后 3、7、14 和 30 天对样品进行测试。
主要结果
所有实验室都正确识别了 ASF 基因组 阳性样本,但只有 85.7% 正确识别了 CSF 基因组 阳性样本。对于血清学检测,大多数实验室表现出较高的准确性,但非洲猪瘟和脑脊液检测的准确性进一步提高。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,虽然 ASF 和 CSF 的分子检测总体上是准确的,但血清学方法仍有改进的空间。该研究强调了能力验证对于确保各实验室诊断性能一致的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为提高 ASF 和 CSF 的诊断准确性提供了重要的见解,这可以为猪群(尤其是美洲的猪群)提供更好的 监测 和控制策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对能力验证练习的依赖以及实验室实践的潜在可变性而受到限制。证据是中等的,因为它是基于绩效评估而不是直接的临床或流行病学结果。

Multi-laboratory proficiency assessment for molecular and serological diagnosis of classical and African swine fever in the Americas

Authors: Onyilagha C, Hochman O, Brawerman G, Goonewardene K, Ambagala A
Original abstract (English)
Given that diagnostic assay performance could vary between laboratories, proficiency testing (PT) exercises play a critical role in ensuring the quality and reliability of laboratory results and in helping laboratories to identify and address procedural issues. Result interpretation: X% ≥ 50%, positive; X% X < 40%, negative; 50% > x% ≥ 40%, suspicious (ASF ELISA kit #1). Bold, ASF-positive; italic, CSF-positive; DPI, days post-inoculation; DPC, days post-challenge; Neg, negative; Pos, positive.Evaluation of PrimeStoreTM MTM for ASFV and CSFV genome stabilityUnlike lyophilized serum, which is generally stable at room temperature for short periods of time, typically lasting from several days to a few weeks, thereby making it suitable for transport at room temperature, viral nucleic acids, especially viral RNA, are not stable beyond 48 h (21) at room temperature, and, if shipped at room temperature, could result in false-negative results when tested by the participating laboratories. Participation, panel distribution, and results reportingAn invitation to participate in the 2024 NCFAD PT exercise for ASF and CSF was sent to the regional and national reference laboratories, as well as local veterinary diagnostic laboratories, in the Americas. African swine fever (ASF) and classical swine fever (CSF) are economically significant transboundary viral diseases of pigs and are reportable to the World Organization for Animal Health (WOAH) due to their severe impact on production and international trade. Both diseases present with similar clinical signs; therefore, they cannot be reliably distinguished without laboratory diagnosis. As one of the WOAH reference laboratories for ASF and CSF, the National Center for Foreign Animal Disease (NCFAD) organized its first PT exercise for...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the diagnostic accuracy of molecular and serological methods for classical swine fever (CSF) and African swine fever (ASF) in the Americas, highlighting the need for standardized testing to ensure reliable results across laboratories.
Design and population
A multi-laboratory proficiency testing exercise was conducted, involving 15 molecular samples (6 ASF and 7 CSF viruses, plus 2 negative controls) and 20 serological samples (10 ASF and 5 CSF antibodies). The study took place in the Americas between 2024 and 2026, with participation from regional and national reference laboratories as well as local veterinary diagnostic laboratories.
Core methods
The molecular panel was prepared using inactivated ASFV and CSFV propagated in porcine primary leukocytes and PK-15 cell lines, respectively. The serological panel included sera from pigs infected with multiple strains of ASFV, CSFV, BDV, or BVDV. Real-time PCR and immunoperoxidase staining were used for molecular detection, while serological testing involved ELISA kits and confirmatory IPT assays. Samples were tested at 3, 7, 14, and 30 days post-treatment with MTM.
Main results
All laboratories correctly identified the ASF genome-positive samples, but only 85.7% correctly identified the CSF genome-positive samples. For serological detection, most laboratories demonstrated high accuracy, but further improvements were noted in ASF and CSF detection.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate that while molecular detection of ASF and CSF was generally accurate, there is room for improvement in serological methods. The study highlights the importance of proficiency testing in ensuring consistent diagnostic performance across laboratories.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide critical insights for improving diagnostic accuracy in ASF and CSF, which can inform better surveillance and control strategies in swine populations, particularly in the Americas.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on proficiency testing exercises and the potential for variability in laboratory practices. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on performance assessments rather than direct clinical or epidemiological outcomes.

打破通道障碍:同荧光、不同Tm探针策略可在20分钟内在六通道PCR平台上实现七靶标呼吸道病毒检测。

Breaking the channel barrier: same-fluorescence, different-Tm probe strategy enables seven-target respiratory virus detection on a six-channel PCR platform within 20 minutes.
作者： Yang Li, Yi-Nuo Zhao, Xiang Li, Ting Sun, Lin-Wei Zhu, Miao-jin Zhu, Li-Guo Liang, Hang-Ping Yao
摘要中文翻译背景 具有重叠症状的呼吸道病原体(包括 SARS-CoV-2、甲型/乙型流感、H5/H7/H10 禽流感 和 RSV)共同循环和共同感染,需要在护理点进行快速多重筛查。传统多重实时PCR受硬件通道容量(≤6)的限制,难以在单管中检测​​7个靶标并区分甲型和乙型流感。这项工作在不牺牲速度或灵敏度的情况下解决了高阶多重和有限荧光通道之间的矛盾。结果我们开发了一个六通道超快速 PCR 平台,使用相同荧光、不同 Tm 探针策略,可在 20 分钟内检测单管中的七种呼吸道病毒。甲型流感和乙型流感共享 FAM 通道,但会产生不同的熔解峰(~12 °C 差异,84.8 °C 与 72.3 °C),从而可以通过熔解曲线分析进行准确分型。引物、探针和酶以冻干珠的形式预先嵌入密封盒中,以实现全自动样品到结果操作。该检测在 5 个拷贝/测试中对所有目标实现了 100% 检测,在 5-105 个拷贝/测试中实现了线性 (R2 > 0.95),效率为 90-110%,并且与 39 种非目标病原体没有交叉反应。墨盒在 25 °C 下可保持稳定 12 个月。对 271 个临床样本的验证显示与测序验证的单重 qPCR 100% 一致。意义和新颖性 这项工作首次将基于熔解曲线的同通道多重技术与冻干柱技术在超快速 PCR 平台上集成。通过克服通道限制,同时保持速度和灵敏度,该系统提供了一种实用的、可现场部署的筛查工具,用于在资源有限的环境中进行分散的呼吸道病原体检测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
具有重叠症状的呼吸道病原体(包括 SARS-CoV-2、甲型/乙型流感、H5/H7/H10 禽流感 和 RSV)共同循环和共同感染,需要在护理点进行快速多重筛查。
设计与对象
传统多重实时PCR受硬件通道容量(≤6)的限制,难以在单管中检测​​7个靶标并区分甲型和乙型流感。
核心方法
这项工作在不牺牲速度或灵敏度的情况下解决了高阶多重和有限荧光通道之间的矛盾。该检测在 5 个拷贝/测试中对所有目标实现了 100% 检测,在 5-105 个拷贝/测试中实现了线性 (R2 > 0.95),效率为 90-110%,并且与 39 种非目标病原体没有交叉反应。引物、探针和酶以冻干珠的形式预先嵌入密封盒中,以实现全自动样品到结果操作。
主要结果
我们开发了一个六通道超快速 PCR 平台,使用相同荧光、不同 Tm 探针策略,可在 20 分钟内检测单管中的 7 种呼吸道病毒。
解释与创新
甲型流感和乙型流感共享 FAM 通道,但会产生不同的熔解峰(~12 °C 差异,84.8 °C 与 72.3 °C),从而可以通过熔解曲线分析进行准确分型。
科研与公卫意义
通过克服通道限制,同时保持速度和灵敏度,该系统提供了一种实用的、可现场部署的筛查工具,用于在资源有限的环境中进行分散的呼吸道病原体检测。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据并未报告研究的局限性或证据强度。

Breaking the channel barrier: same-fluorescence, different-Tm probe strategy enables seven-target respiratory virus detection on a six-channel PCR platform within 20 minutes.

Authors: Yang Li, Yi-Nuo Zhao, Xiang Li, Ting Sun, Lin-Wei Zhu, Miao-jin Zhu, Li-Guo Liang, Hang-Ping Yao
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Respiratory pathogens with overlapping symptoms-including SARS-CoV-2, influenza A/B, H5/H7/H10 avian influenza, and RSV-co-circulate and co-infect, demanding rapid multiplex screening at points of care. Conventional multiplex real-time PCR is constrained by hardware channel capacity (≤6), making it difficult to detect seven targets while distinguishing influenza A and B in a single tube. This work addresses the contradiction between high-order multiplexing and limited fluorescence channels without sacrificing speed or sensitivity. RESULTS We developed a six-channel ultra-fast PCR platform detecting seven respiratory viruses in a single tube within 20 min, using a same-fluorescence, different-Tm probe strategy. Influenza A and B share the FAM channel yet generate distinct melting peaks (∼12 °C difference, 84.8 °C vs. 72.3 °C), enabling accurate typing via melting curve analysis. Primers, probes, and enzymes are pre-embedded as lyophilized beads in sealed cartridges for fully automated sample-to-result operation. The assay achieved 100% detection at 5 copies/test for all targets, linearity (R2 > 0.95) across 5-105 copies/test, 90-110% efficiencies, and no cross-reactivity against 39 non-target pathogens. Cartridges remained stable for 12 months at 25 °C. Validation on 271 clinical specimens showed 100% agreement with sequencing-verified single-plex qPCR. SIGNIFICANCE AND NOVELTY This work provides the first integration of melting-curve-based same-channel multiplexing with lyophilized cartridge technology on an ultra-fast PCR platform. By overcoming channel limitations while maintaining speed and sensitivity, the system offers a practical, field-deployable screening tool for decentralized respiratory pathogen detection in resource-limited settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory pathogens with overlapping symptoms-including SARS-CoV-2, influenza A/B, H5/H7/H10 avian influenza, and RSV-co-circulate and co-infect, demanding rapid multiplex screening at points of care.

Core methods

The assay achieved 100% detection at 5 copies/test for all targets, linearity (R2 > 0.95) across 5-105 copies/test, 90-110% efficiencies, and no cross-reactivity against 39 non-target pathogens. Primers, probes, and enzymes are pre-embedded as lyophilized beads in sealed cartridges for fully automated sample-to-result operation.
Main results

Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength for the study.

空间解析传染病模型在局部暴发缓解中的应用(以及高效的数学软件)

Spatially resolved infectious disease models for localized outbreak mitigation (and efficient mathematical software)
作者： Kühn, Martin Joachim
摘要中文翻译有其他应用场景) 因素100 因素100-1000 软件技术 数值方法 让我们创造更多的跨学科协同作用 软件技术 数值方法 公共卫生 免疫学 流行病学 推断10 丢失空间解析:原因和机会 11 丢失空间解析:原因和机会 12 丢失空间解析:原因和机会 13 丢失空间解析:原因和机会 14 丢失空间解析:原因和机会 而这仅仅是2304个CPU核心... 30天模拟时间的中位结果。• T0,T1,T2,T5:无测试,每周测试一次、两次或五次 • L:封锁 • D1W,D3W:干预措施的实施,延迟一周或三周 16 应用:无Covid控制策略的数值评估 Kühn等人,BMC传染病(2022) https://doi.org/10.1186/s12879-022-07302-9 • T0,T1,T2,T5:无测试,每周测试一次、两次或五次 • L,L+:封锁或严格封锁 • D1W,D3W:干预措施的实施,延迟一周或三周 29 基于ODE的元种群模型,带有反馈机制 • 融合空间解析的人类行为 • 基于感知风险的反馈回路,带有记忆核 • 额定ICU容量作为调节措施的阈值 • 调节更新有效接触率 https://doi.org/10.1016/j.chaos.2025.116782 Zunker等人,混沌、孤子与分形(2025) Henrik Zunker 基于旅行时间的Graph-ODE元种群扩展 30 Zunker等人,PLOS计算生物学(2024) https://doi.org/10.1371/journal.pcbi.1012630 Henrik Zunker 基于AI的在线计算用于Web应用程序 31 • 模拟空间解析的专家模型 • 在专家模型上训练空间解析的AI代理(图神经网络)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
研究旨在开发空间解析传染病模型以实现局部暴发缓解,并高效数学软件,通过先进的建模技术提高大流行准备能力。
设计与对象
该研究涉及 SARS-CoV-2 病例的模拟传播,初始情景包括约 18% 的红色区域和 8 种不同的策略。对于 2-20% 的县,随机初始 发病率 设置为每 100,000 人每周有 75-150 例病例,否则 发病率 低于 10。
核心方法
研究采用了混合代理-元种群模型,结合了焦点区域的代理模型和连接区域的ODE模型。还包括一个具有8000个代理、1%初始感染率和基于区域的不同传播率的证明概念空间混合模型。
主要结果
模拟时间30天后的中位数结果显示,空间混合模型在40,000个代理的情况下,将运行时间减少了98%,同时保持了与基于代理的模型相似的扩展行为。
解释与创新
作者解释结果表明,空间混合模型在必要时能够捕捉到随机性和个体行为的效率,同时利用ODE模型在连接区域的计算优势。这种方法代表了不同建模技术在流行病控制中的新颖整合。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以指导更高效和准确的模型开发,特别是在计算资源有限的场景中,支持在大流行准备中使用空间解析模型。
局限与证据强度
研究受限于对模拟数据的依赖和缺乏实地验证。证据为中等,因为它提出了一个概念模型并讨论了对未来研究和应用的含义。

Spatially resolved infectious disease models for localized outbreak mitigation (and efficient mathematical software)

Authors: Kühn, Martin Joachim

View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop spatially resolved infectious disease models for localized outbreak mitigation and efficient mathematical software, addressing the need for improved pandemic preparedness through advanced modeling techniques.
Design and population
The study involved a simulated spread of SARS-CoV-2 cases for one initial scenario with about 18% red zones and 8 different strategies. Random initial incidence was set at 75-150 weekly cases per 100,000 individuals for 2-20% of the counties, with incidence below 10 otherwise.
Core methods
The study employed a hybrid agent-metapopulation model, combining agent-based models in the focus region with ODE-based models for connected regions. It also included a proof-of-concept spatial hybrid model with 8000 agents, 1% initially infected, and different transmission rates based on regions.
Main results
The median result after 30 days of simulation time showed that the spatial-hybrid model reduced runtime by 98% for 40,000 agents compared to agent-based models, while maintaining similar scaling behavior.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the results as demonstrating the efficiency of the spatial-hybrid model in capturing stochasticity and individual behavior when necessary, while leveraging the computational advantages of ODE-based models for connected regions. This approach represents a novel integration of different modeling techniques for epidemic control.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more efficient and accurate models for outbreak mitigation, particularly in scenarios with limited computational resources, and support the use of spatially resolved models in pandemic preparedness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on simulated data and the absence of real-world validation. The evidence is moderate as it presents a proof-of-concept model and discusses implications for future research and applications.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

H3 亚型 禽流感 病毒:生态、跨物种 进化 和公共健康威胁

H3 Subtype Avian Influenza Virus: Ecology, Cross-Species Evolution, and Public Health Threats
作者： Kai-Hui Cheng, Zhi-Jun Yu
摘要中文翻译H3 亚型 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 是全球 野生鸟类 和家禽中分布最广泛、最常检测到的低致病性 禽流感 病毒 (LPAIV) 之一。野生水禽是该病毒的天然储存宿主。近年来,在频繁的基因重排和持续的进化的推动下,H3亚型AIV已将其宿主范围扩大到包括各种哺乳动物物种——狗、猫、马、海豹、猪(实验感染)和人类。其中,H3N8亚型表现出最广泛的跨物种传播性,在马和狗中建立了持续的传播谱系,同时也在海豹和人类中引起散发性感染。近二十年来,禽源 H3N2 犬流感病毒在亚洲和北美的狗群中稳定传播。2022年至2023年期间,中国报告了三例人感染H3N8亚型AIV病例,其中一例死亡(广东,2023年3月),表明该病毒具有潜在的人畜共患传播风险。新型三重重组毒株不断出现,包括 2022 年底首次在鸡群中检测到的 H3N8 和 H3N3 基因型 G25。它们的HA基因源自野生鸟类或鸡源的H3亚型病毒,而其内部基因片段主要从H9N2亚型AIV获得,从而增强了哺乳动物的适应性。本综述总结了H3亚型AIV的全球流行病学(涵盖野生鸟类、家禽和活禽市场)、其遗传进化和重配模式,以及跨物种传播的分子机制。本综述特别强调了关键氨基酸 突变(例如 PB2-D701N、M1-M192V 和 HA-Q226L/G228S)在受体结合偏好、聚合酶活性和致病性中的调节作用。本综述还总结了检测技术(多重 RT-PCR、基于量子点的免疫层析试纸条、RT-RAA 和 CRISPR-Cas13a)和 疫苗 开发(灭活、腺病毒载体和 mRNA 疫苗)的最新进展。还讨论了关键的预防和控制挑战,包括秘密循环、监测 差距以及初级实验室有限的亚型诊断能力。本次审查建议,H3 亚型 AIV 应被视为潜在的人畜共患威胁,需要继续实施 监测,尽管迄今为止尚未有证据表明持续存在人与人之间的 传播。需要更好地了解跨物种机制、更强的 监测 和早期预警以及基于证据的控制策略,以便为未来的风险评估提供信息。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
H3 亚型 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 是全球 野生鸟类 和家禽中分布最广泛、最常检测到的低致病性 禽流感 病毒 (LPAIV) 之一。野生水禽是该病毒的天然储存宿主。本综述总结了H3亚型AIV的全球流行病学(涵盖野生鸟类、家禽和活禽市场)、其遗传进化和重配模式,以及跨物种传播的分子机制。本综述特别强调了关键氨基酸 突变(例如 PB2-D701N、M1-M192V 和 HA-Q226L/G228S)在受体结合偏好、聚合酶活性和致病性中的调节作用。
证据基础与方法
本综述还总结了检测技术(多重 RT-PCR、基于量子点的免疫层析试纸条、RT-RAA 和 CRISPR-Cas13a)和 疫苗 开发(灭活、腺病毒载体和 mRNA 疫苗)的最新进展。
共识与结论
2022年至2023年期间,中国报告了三例人感染H3N8亚型AIV病例,其中一例死亡(广东,2023年3月),表明该病毒具有潜在的人畜共患传播风险。新型三重重组毒株不断出现,包括 2022 年底首次在鸡群中检测到的 H3N8 和 H3N3 基因型 G25。它们的HA基因源自野生鸟类或鸡源的H3亚型病毒,而其内部基因片段主要从H9N2亚型AIV获得,从而增强了哺乳动物的适应性。
争议与缺口
还讨论了关键的预防和控制挑战,包括秘密循环、监测 差距以及初级实验室有限的亚型诊断能力。
科研与实践启示
本次审查建议,H3 亚型 AIV 应被视为潜在的人畜共患威胁,需要继续实施 监测,尽管迄今为止尚未有证据表明持续存在人与人之间的 传播。需要更好地了解跨物种机制、更强的 监测 和早期预警以及基于证据的控制策略,以便为未来的风险评估提供信息。

H3 Subtype Avian Influenza Virus: Ecology, Cross-Species Evolution, and Public Health Threats

Authors: Kai-Hui Cheng, Zhi-Jun Yu
Original abstract (English)
H3 subtype avian influenza virus (AIV) is one of the most widely distributed and frequently detected low-pathogenicity avian influenza viruses (LPAIVs) in wild birds and poultry worldwide. Wild waterfowl are the natural reservoir of this virus. In recent years, driven by frequent genetic reassortment and continuous evolution, H3 subtype AIV has expanded its host range to include various mammalian species—dogs, cats, horses, seals, pigs (experimental infection), and humans. Among these, the H3N8 subtype shows the broadest cross-species transmissibility, having established sustained transmission lineages in horses and dogs, while also causing sporadic infections in seals and humans. Avian-origin H3N2 canine influenza virus has circulated stably in dog populations across Asia and North America for nearly two decades. Between 2022 and 2023, China reported three human cases of H3N8 subtype AIV infection, one of which was fatal (Guangdong, March 2023), indicating that this virus poses a potential zoonotic transmission risk. Novel triple-reassortant strains, including genotype G25 of H3N8 and H3N3, first detected in chicken flocks in late 2022, have continued to emerge. Their HA genes originate from H3 subtype viruses of wild bird or chicken origin, whereas their internal gene segments are predominantly acquired from H9N2 subtype AIV, resulting in enhanced mammalian adaptability. This review summarizes the global epidemiology of H3 subtype AIV (covering wild birds, poultry, and live poultry markets), its genetic evolution and reassortment patterns, and the molecular mechanisms underlying cross-species transmission. This review particularly highlights the regulatory roles of key amino acid mutations—such as PB2-D701N, M1-M192V, and HA-Q226L/G228S—in receptor binding preference, polymerase activity, and pathogenicity. This review also summarizes recent advances in detection techniques (multiplex RT-PCR, quantum dot-based immunochromatographic strips, RT-RAA, and CRISPR-Cas13a) and vaccine development (inactivated, adenovirus-vectored, and mRNA vaccines). Key prevention and control challenges—including covert circulation, surveillance gaps, and limited subtyping diagnostic capacity at primary laboratories—are also discussed. This review proposes that H3 subtype AIV should be recognized as a potential zoonotic threat warranting continued surveillance, although no evidence of sustained human-to-human transmission has been documented to date. Better understanding of cross-species mechanisms, stronger surveillance and early warning, and evidence-based control strategies are needed to inform future risk assessment.
